### [75] [A Multi-Stage Optimization Pipeline for Bethesda Cell Detection in Pap Smear Cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13939)
*Martin Amster,Camila María Polotto*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于YOLO和U-Net集成的新框架，用于Pap涂片图像中Bethesda细胞检测，在Riva细胞学挑战赛Track B中获得第二名，mAP50-95得分为0.5909


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机视觉技术在医学领域应用日益广泛，但Pap涂片图像中Bethesda细胞的准确检测仍具挑战性。Riva细胞学挑战赛为评估细胞检测模型性能提供了平台，本文旨在开发高性能的细胞检测框架

Method: 提出集成YOLO和U-Net架构的框架，采用重叠去除技术和二元分类器进行细化。YOLO负责目标检测，U-Net用于分割，通过集成策略结合两者优势，后处理阶段优化检测结果

Result: 在Riva细胞学挑战赛Track B中获得第二名，mAP50-95得分为0.5909。框架性能优于单一模型，验证了集成方法和后处理技术的有效性

Conclusion: 集成YOLO和U-Net的框架能有效提升Pap涂片图像中Bethesda细胞的检测性能，为医学图像分析提供了实用解决方案，代码已开源供研究使用

Abstract: Computer vision techniques have advanced significantly in recent years, finding diverse and impactful applications within the medical field. In this paper, we introduce a new framework for the detection of Bethesda cells in Pap smear images, developed for Track B of the Riva Cytology Challenge held in association with the International Symposium on Biomedical Imaging (ISBI). This work focuses on enhancing computer vision models for cell detection, with performance evaluated using the mAP50-95 metric. We propose a solution based on an ensemble of YOLO and U-Net architectures, followed by a refinement stage utilizing overlap removal techniques and a binary classifier. Our framework achieved second place with a mAP50-95 score of 0.5909 in the competition. The implementation and source code are available at the following repository: github.com/martinamster/riva-trackb

</details>


### [76] [SceneGlue: Scene-Aware Transformer for Feature Matching without Scene-Level Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13941)
*Songlin Du,Xiaoyong Lu,Yaping Yan,Guobao Xiao,Xiaobo Lu,Takeshi Ikenaga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SceneGlue是一个场景感知的特征匹配框架，通过混合化匹配范式结合隐式并行注意力和显式跨视图可见性估计，克服传统局部特征描述符的限制，提升跨视图图像对应关系的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部特征匹配方法受限于特征描述符的局部性质，难以捕捉对准确跨视图对应关系至关重要的非局部场景信息。需要一种能够理解场景全局上下文和可见性关系的匹配框架。

Method: 提出SceneGlue框架，采用混合化匹配范式：1) 隐式并行注意力机制，在图像内部和跨图像间同时交换局部描述符信息，增强全局上下文；2) 显式可见性变换器，将特征分类为可见和不可见区域，理解跨视图场景可见性。仅使用局部特征匹配进行训练，无需场景级标注。

Result: 在单应性估计、姿态估计、图像匹配和视觉定位等应用中进行了广泛实验，验证了SceneGlue的优越性能。相比传统方法，不仅提高了准确性和鲁棒性，还增强了可解释性。

Conclusion: SceneGlue通过结合显式和隐式场景级感知，有效补偿了局部描述符的局限性，为跨视图图像对应关系提供了更准确、鲁棒且可解释的解决方案。代码已开源。

Abstract: Local feature matching plays a critical role in understanding the correspondence between cross-view images. However, traditional methods are constrained by the inherent local nature of feature descriptors, limiting their ability to capture non-local scene information that is essential for accurate cross-view correspondence. In this paper, we introduce SceneGlue, a scene-aware feature matching framework designed to overcome these limitations. SceneGlue leverages a hybridizable matching paradigm that integrates implicit parallel attention and explicit cross-view visibility estimation. The parallel attention mechanism simultaneously exchanges information among local descriptors within and across images, enhancing the scene's global context. To further enrich the scene awareness, we propose the Visibility Transformer, which explicitly categorizes features into visible and invisible regions, providing an understanding of cross-view scene visibility. By combining explicit and implicit scene-level awareness, SceneGlue effectively compensates for the local descriptor constraints. Notably, SceneGlue is trained using only local feature matches, without requiring scene-level groundtruth annotations. This scene-aware approach not only improves accuracy and robustness but also enhances interpretability compared to traditional methods. Extensive experiments on applications such as homography estimation, pose estimation, image matching, and visual localization validate SceneGlue's superior performance. The source code is available at https://github.com/songlin-du/SceneGlue.

</details>


### [77] [Heuristic Style Transfer for Real-Time, Efficient Weather Attribute Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13947)
*Hamed Ouattara,Pierre Duthon,Pascal Houssam Salmane,Frédéric Bernardin,Omar Ait Aider*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出轻量高效的天气条件检测架构，通过风格分析方法预测天气类型和11个补充属性，包含RTM和PMG两个架构家族，在零样本评估中表现出良好泛化能力，适用于嵌入式系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究天气条件在多大程度上表现为视觉风格变化，开发能够实时运行且内存占用小的天气检测系统，适用于嵌入式设备应用。

Method: 采用风格启发技术，包括Gram矩阵、截断ResNet-50（针对低层和中间层）和PatchGAN风格架构，构建多任务框架并集成注意力机制。提出RTM（ResNet50截断多任务）和PMG（PatchGAN多任务Gram）两个架构家族及其变体。

Result: 模型在内部测试集上F1分数超过96%，在多个外部数据集上的零样本评估中超过78%，验证了泛化能力。PMG架构参数少于500万，实时运行且内存占用小。发布了包含503,875张图像和12个天气属性的数据集。

Conclusion: 天气条件确实表现为视觉风格变化，可以通过风格分析方法有效检测。提出的轻量级架构在保持高精度的同时实现了实时性能和低内存占用，模块化设计支持任务灵活配置，适用于嵌入式系统部署。

Abstract: We present lightweight and efficient architectures to detect weather conditions from RGB images, predicting the weather type (sunny, rain, snow, fog) and 11 complementary attributes such as intensity, visibility, and ground condition, for a total of 53 classes across the tasks. This work examines to what extent weather conditions manifest as variations in visual style. We investigate style-inspired techniques, including Gram matrices, a truncated ResNet-50 targeting lower and intermediate layers, and PatchGAN-style architectures, within a multi-task framework with attention mechanisms. Two families are introduced: RTM (ResNet50-Truncated-MultiTasks) and PMG (PatchGAN-MultiTasks-Gram), together with their variants. Our contributions include automation of Gram-matrix computation, integration of PatchGAN into supervised multi-task learning, and local style capture through local Gram for improved spatial coherence. We also release a dataset of 503,875 images annotated with 12 weather attributes under a Creative Commons Attribution (CC-BY) license. The models achieve F1 scores above 96 percent on our internal test set and above 78 percent in zero-shot evaluation on several external datasets, confirming their generalization ability. The PMG architecture, with fewer than 5 million parameters, runs in real time with a small memory footprint, making it suitable for embedded systems. The modular design of the models also allows style-related or weather-related tasks to be added or removed as needed.

</details>


### [78] [MApLe: Multi-instance Alignment of Diagnostic Reports and Large Medical Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13970)
*Felicia Bader,Philipp Seeböck,Anastasia Bartashova,Ulrike Attenberger,Georg Langs*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MApLe是一种多任务、多实例视觉语言对齐方法，专门针对医学诊断报告中微小图像区域与文本描述的关联问题，通过解耦解剖区域和诊断发现概念，实现图像补丁与句子的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学诊断报告将复杂的影像数据编码为临床可操作信息，描述解剖背景下的细微病理发现。标准视觉语言模型难以识别这些信息丰富的文本成分与图像中微小但重要的位置之间的关联。

Method: 提出MApLe方法：1) 训练文本嵌入捕获句子中的解剖和诊断概念；2) 基于解剖结构条件的补丁级图像编码器；3) 这些表示的多实例对齐。该方法解耦解剖区域和诊断发现概念，采用补丁级方法将局部图像信息与句子关联。

Result: MApLe能够成功对齐不同图像区域和自由文本报告中的多个诊断发现。在多个下游任务评估中，相比最先进的基线模型，MApLe提高了对齐性能。

Conclusion: MApLe通过多任务、多实例视觉语言对齐方法，有效解决了医学诊断报告中微小图像区域与文本描述的关联问题，在医学影像分析领域具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: In diagnostic reports, experts encode complex imaging data into clinically actionable information. They describe subtle pathological findings that are meaningful in their anatomical context. Reports follow relatively consistent structures, expressing diagnostic information with few words that are often associated with tiny but consequential image observations. Standard vision language models struggle to identify the associations between these informative text components and small locations in the images. Here, we propose "MApLe", a multi-task, multi-instance vision language alignment approach that overcomes these limitations. It disentangles the concepts of anatomical region and diagnostic finding, and links local image information to sentences in a patch-wise approach. Our method consists of a text embedding trained to capture anatomical and diagnostic concepts in sentences, a patch-wise image encoder conditioned on anatomical structures, and a multi-instance alignment of these representations. We demonstrate that MApLe can successfully align different image regions and multiple diagnostic findings in free-text reports. We show that our model improves the alignment performance compared to state-of-the-art baseline models when evaluated on several downstream tasks. The code is available at https://github.com/cirmuw/MApLe.

</details>


### [79] [HiProto: Hierarchical Prototype Learning for Interpretable Object Detection Under Low-quality Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13981)
*Jianlin Xiang,Linhui Dai,Xue Yang,Chaolei Yang,Yanshan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HiProto：基于分层原型学习的可解释目标检测新范式，通过构建多特征层次的结构化原型表示来增强语义区分和可解释性，特别适用于低质量成像条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关键应用中部署目标检测系统时，可解释性至关重要，尤其是在低质量成像条件下，视觉信息退化会增加预测不确定性。现有方法要么增强图像质量，要么设计复杂架构，但往往缺乏可解释性且未能改善语义区分。原型学习通过将特征与类中心语义关联，能够在退化条件下提供更稳定和可解释的表示。

Method: 提出HiProto，一种基于分层原型学习的可解释目标检测范式。主要包含三个核心组件：1) 区域到原型对比损失（RPC-Loss），增强原型对目标区域的语义关注；2) 原型正则化损失（PR-Loss），提高类原型之间的区分度；3) 尺度感知伪标签生成策略（SPLGS），抑制RPC-Loss中的不匹配监督，保持低层原型表示的鲁棒性。

Result: 在ExDark、RTTS和VOC2012-FOG数据集上的实验表明，HiProto在保持竞争力的检测性能的同时，通过原型响应提供了清晰的可解释性，且不依赖图像增强或复杂架构。

Conclusion: HiProto通过分层原型学习为可解释目标检测提供了新范式，在低质量成像条件下实现了语义区分和可解释性的双重提升，为关键应用中的可靠目标检测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Interpretability is essential for deploying object detection systems in critical applications, especially under low-quality imaging conditions that degrade visual information and increase prediction uncertainty. Existing methods either enhance image quality or design complex architectures, but often lack interpretability and fail to improve semantic discrimination. In contrast, prototype learning enables interpretable modeling by associating features with class-centered semantics, which can provide more stable and interpretable representations under degradation. Motivated by this, we propose HiProto, a new paradigm for interpretable object detection based on hierarchical prototype learning. By constructing structured prototype representations across multiple feature levels, HiProto effectively models class-specific semantics, thereby enhancing both semantic discrimination and interpretability. Building upon prototype modeling, we first propose a Region-to-Prototype Contrastive Loss (RPC-Loss) to enhance the semantic focus of prototypes on target regions. Then, we propose a Prototype Regularization Loss (PR-Loss) to improve the distinctiveness among class prototypes. Finally, we propose a Scale-aware Pseudo Label Generation Strategy (SPLGS) to suppress mismatched supervision for RPC-Loss, thereby preserving the robustness of low-level prototype representations. Experiments on ExDark, RTTS, and VOC2012-FOG demonstrate that HiProto achieves competitive results while offering clear interpretability through prototype responses, without relying on image enhancement or complex architectures. Our code will be available at https://github.com/xjlDestiny/HiProto.git.

</details>


### [80] [Remote Sensing Image Super-Resolution for Imbalanced Textures: A Texture-Aware Diffusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13994)
*Enzhuo Zhang,Sijie Zhao,Dilxat Muhtar,Zhenshi Li,Xueliang Zhang,Pengfeng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TexADiff是一种针对遥感图像超分辨率的纹理感知扩散框架，通过相对纹理密度图来引导扩散过程，解决遥感图像纹理分布不平衡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成扩散先验在自然图像超分辨率中表现出色，但直接应用于遥感图像超分辨率存在显著不足。遥感图像具有独特的纹理分布特征：地面物体全局随机但局部聚集，导致高度不平衡的纹理分布，严重阻碍模型的空间感知能力。

Method: 提出TexADiff框架：1）首先估计相对纹理密度图来表示纹理分布；2）将RTDM作为显式空间条件引导扩散过程；3）作为损失调制项优先处理纹理丰富区域；4）作为采样计划的动态适配器。这些修改赋予模型显式的纹理感知能力。

Result: 实验表明TexADiff在定量指标上达到优越或竞争性水平。定性结果显示模型能够生成忠实的高频细节，同时有效抑制纹理幻觉。改进的重建质量还带来了下游任务性能的显著提升。

Conclusion: TexADiff通过纹理感知的扩散框架成功解决了遥感图像超分辨率中的纹理不平衡问题，在保持高频细节真实性的同时抑制幻觉，为遥感图像处理提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Generative diffusion priors have recently achieved state-of-the-art performance in natural image super-resolution, demonstrating a powerful capability to synthesize photorealistic details. However, their direct application to remote sensing image super-resolution (RSISR) reveals significant shortcomings. Unlike natural images, remote sensing images exhibit a unique texture distribution where ground objects are globally stochastic yet locally clustered, leading to highly imbalanced textures. This imbalance severely hinders the model's spatial perception. To address this, we propose TexADiff, a novel framework that begins by estimating a Relative Texture Density Map (RTDM) to represent the texture distribution. TexADiff then leverages this RTDM in three synergistic ways: as an explicit spatial conditioning to guide the diffusion process, as a loss modulation term to prioritize texture-rich regions, and as a dynamic adapter for the sampling schedule. These modifications are designed to endow the model with explicit texture-aware capabilities. Experiments demonstrate that TexADiff achieves superior or competitive quantitative metrics. Furthermore, qualitative results show that our model generates faithful high-frequency details while effectively suppressing texture hallucinations. This improved reconstruction quality also results in significant gains in downstream task performance. The source code of our method can be found at https://github.com/ZezFuture/TexAdiff.

</details>


### [81] [Depth-Aware Image and Video Orientation Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13995)
*Muhammad Z. Alam,Larry Stetsiuk,M. Umair Mukati,Zeeshan Kaleem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于深度分布估计图像和视频方向的新方法，通过深度梯度一致性和水平对称性分析增强感知对齐，在VR/AR等应用中表现优异


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像方向估计方法可能缺乏对深度信息的充分利用，特别是在需要空间一致性和感知稳定性的沉浸式视觉应用中。本文旨在利用自然图像中的深度分布特征来提供更鲁棒的方向估计框架。

Method: 基于图像不同象限的深度分布进行方向估计，结合深度梯度一致性（DGC）和水平对称性分析（HSA）来增强细粒度感知对齐，形成混合策略以利用深度线索支持空间一致性和感知稳定性。

Result: 定性和定量评估表明该方法具有鲁棒性和准确性，在多样化场景中优于现有技术，特别适用于虚拟现实、增强现实、自主导航和交互式监控系统等应用。

Conclusion: 通过深度分布和感知对齐技术的结合，本文提出的方法为图像和视频方向估计提供了有效的解决方案，在沉浸式视觉内容中实现了更好的空间一致性和感知稳定性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel approach for image and video orientation estimation by leveraging depth distribution in natural images. The proposed method estimates the orientation based on the depth distribution across different quadrants of the image, providing a robust framework for orientation estimation suited for applications such as virtual reality (VR), augmented reality (AR), autonomous navigation, and interactive surveillance systems. To further enhance fine-scale perceptual alignment, we incorporate depth gradient consistency (DGC) and horizontal symmetry analysis (HSA), enabling precise orientation correction. This hybrid strategy effectively exploits depth cues to support spatial coherence and perceptual stability in immersive visual content. Qualitative and quantitative evaluations demonstrate the robustness and accuracy of the proposed approach, outperforming existing techniques across diverse scenarios.

</details>


### [82] [Decoding the Delta: Unifying Remote Sensing Change Detection and Understanding with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14044)
*Xiaohe Li,Jiahao Li,Kaixin Zhang,Yuqiang Fang,Leilei Lin,Hong Wang,Haohua Wu,Zide Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Delta-LLaVA：针对遥感变化理解的新型多模态大语言模型框架，通过Delta-QA基准和三个核心创新模块解决MLLM的"时间盲区"问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大语言模型在通用视觉-语言任务上表现出色，但在遥感变化理解中存在"时间盲区"问题，缺乏多时间对比推理的内在机制和精确的空间定位能力

Method: 提出Delta-LLaVA框架，包含三个核心创新：1) Change-Enhanced Attention模块系统隔离和放大视觉差异；2) Change-SEG模块利用Change Prior Embedding提取可微分的差异特征作为LLM输入；3) Local Causal Attention防止跨时间上下文泄漏

Result: Delta-LLaVA在复杂变化推理和高精度边界定位方面显著优于领先的通用MLLM和专用分割模型，建立了地球观测智能的统一框架

Conclusion: Delta-LLaVA通过专门设计的架构解决了MLLM在遥感变化理解中的根本限制，为多时间遥感解释提供了有效的统一解决方案

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) excel in general vision-language tasks, their application to remote sensing change understanding is hindered by a fundamental "temporal blindness". Existing architectures lack intrinsic mechanisms for multi-temporal contrastive reasoning and struggle with precise spatial grounding. To address this, we first introduce Delta-QA, a comprehensive benchmark comprising 180k visual question-answering samples. Delta-QA unifies pixel-level segmentation and visual question answering across bi- and tri-temporal scenarios, structuring change interpretation into four progressive cognitive dimensions. Methodologically, we propose Delta-LLaVA, a novel MLLM framework explicitly tailored for multi-temporal remote sensing interpretation. It overcomes the limitations of naive feature concatenation through three core innovations: a Change-Enhanced Attention module that systematically isolates and amplifies visual differences, a Change-SEG module utilizing Change Prior Embedding to extract differentiable difference features as input for the LLM, and Local Causal Attention to prevent cross-temporal contextual leakage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Delta-LLaVA decisively outperforms leading generalist MLLMs and specialized segmentation models in complex change deduction and high-precision boundary localization, establishing a unified framework for earth observation intelligence.

</details>


### [83] [Free Geometry: Refining 3D Reconstruction from Longer Versions of Itself](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14048)
*Yuhang Dai,Xingyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Free Geometry框架让前馈式3D重建模型能够在测试时自适应进化，通过跨视图特征一致性自监督和轻量级LoRA更新，无需3D真值即可提升遮挡、镜面反射等困难场景的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前馈式3D重建模型虽然高效但缺乏适应性：一旦训练完成，只能进行零样本推理，无法根据测试场景进行调整。这导致在遮挡、镜面反射和模糊线索等情况下经常出现错误的重建结果。

Method: 提出Free Geometry框架，核心洞察是模型接收更多视图时会产生更可靠且视图一致的重建。在测试序列中，通过掩码部分帧构建自监督任务，强制全观测和部分观测之间的跨视图特征一致性，同时保持被遮挡帧隐含的成对关系。使用轻量级LoRA更新进行快速重新校准，单GPU上每个数据集耗时不到2分钟。

Result: 在4个基准数据集上持续改进了包括Depth Anything 3和VGGT在内的最先进基础模型，相机姿态精度平均提升3.73%，点云图预测平均提升2.88%。

Conclusion: Free Geometry框架成功实现了前馈式3D重建模型在测试时的自适应进化，通过自监督学习和轻量级更新机制，显著提升了在困难场景下的重建质量，为3D重建领域提供了新的测试时自适应范式。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D reconstruction models are efficient but rigid: once trained, they perform inference in a zero-shot manner and cannot adapt to the test scene. As a result, visually plausible reconstructions often contain errors, particularly under occlusions, specularities, and ambiguous cues. To address this, we introduce Free Geometry, a framework that enables feed-forward 3D reconstruction models to self-evolve at test time without any 3D ground truth. Our key insight is that, when the model receives more views, it produces more reliable and view-consistent reconstructions. Leveraging this property, given a testing sequence, we mask a subset of frames to construct a self-supervised task. Free Geometry enforces cross-view feature consistency between representations from full and partial observations, while maintaining the pairwise relations implied by the held-out frames. This self-supervision allows for fast recalibration via lightweight LoRA updates, taking less than 2 minutes per dataset on a single GPU. Our approach consistently improves state-of-the-art foundation models, including Depth Anything 3 and VGGT, across 4 benchmark datasets, yielding an average improvement of 3.73% in camera pose accuracy and 2.88% in point map prediction. Code is available at https://github.com/hiteacherIamhumble/Free-Geometry .

</details>


### [84] [OneHOI: Unifying Human-Object Interaction Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14062)
*Jiun Tian Hoe,Weipeng Hu,Xudong Jiang,Yap-Peng Tan,Chee Seng Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneHOI是一个统一的扩散Transformer框架，将HOI生成和编辑整合到单个条件去噪过程中，通过共享的结构化交互表示实现混合条件控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HOI建模方法存在局限性：HOI生成方法无法整合混合条件（如HOI和仅对象实体），而HOI编辑方法难以解耦姿势与物理接触，且难以扩展到多个交互。需要统一的框架来解决这些问题。

Method: 提出OneHOI框架，核心是关系扩散Transformer（R-DiT），通过角色和实例感知的HOI令牌、基于布局的空间动作接地、结构化HOI注意力来强制执行交互拓扑，以及HOI RoPE来解耦多HOI场景。使用模态丢弃在HOI-Edit-44K数据集上进行联合训练。

Result: OneHOI支持布局引导、无布局、任意掩码和混合条件控制，在HOI生成和编辑任务上都达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: OneHOI提供了一个统一的框架，成功整合了HOI生成和编辑能力，通过共享的结构化交互表示解决了现有方法的局限性，实现了灵活多样的条件控制。

Abstract: Human-Object Interaction (HOI) modelling captures how humans act upon and relate to objects, typically expressed as <person, action, object> triplets. Existing approaches split into two disjoint families: HOI generation synthesises scenes from structured triplets and layout, but fails to integrate mixed conditions like HOI and object-only entities; and HOI editing modifies interactions via text, yet struggles to decouple pose from physical contact and scale to multiple interactions. We introduce OneHOI, a unified diffusion transformer framework that consolidates HOI generation and editing into a single conditional denoising process driven by shared structured interaction representations. At its core, the Relational Diffusion Transformer (R-DiT) models verb-mediated relations through role- and instance-aware HOI tokens, layout-based spatial Action Grounding, a Structured HOI Attention to enforce interaction topology, and HOI RoPE to disentangle multi-HOI scenes. Trained jointly with modality dropout on our HOI-Edit-44K, along with HOI and object-centric datasets, OneHOI supports layout-guided, layout-free, arbitrary-mask, and mixed-condition control, achieving state-of-the-art results across both HOI generation and editing. Code is available at https://jiuntian.github.io/OneHOI/.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Unconstrained Human-Object Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14069)
*Francesco Tonini,Alessandro Conti,Lorenzo Vaquero,Cigdem Beyan,Elisa Ricci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出无约束人-物交互检测任务，利用多模态大语言模型实现无需预定义交互词汇的开放世界交互识别


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人-物交互检测模型依赖预定义的交互词汇表，限制了其在动态开放环境中的应用。多模态大语言模型的出现为实现更灵活的交互识别范式提供了可能

Method: 定义无约束人-物交互任务，评估多种多模态大语言模型在该任务上的表现，提出包含测试时推理和语言到图转换的流水线，从自由文本中提取结构化交互信息

Result: 研究揭示了当前人-物交互检测器的局限性，并证明了多模态大语言模型在无约束人-物交互任务中的价值

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型为开放世界人-物交互检测提供了新的解决方案，无约束人-物交互任务代表了该领域的重要发展方向

Abstract: Human-Object Interaction (HOI) detection is a longstanding computer vision problem concerned with predicting the interaction between humans and objects. Current HOI models rely on a vocabulary of interactions at training and inference time, limiting their applicability to static environments. With the advent of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), it has become feasible to explore more flexible paradigms for interaction recognition. In this work, we revisit HOI detection through the lens of MLLMs and apply them to in-the-wild HOI detection. We define the Unconstrained HOI (U-HOI) task, a novel HOI domain that removes the requirement for a predefined list of interactions at both training and inference. We evaluate a range of MLLMs on this setting and introduce a pipeline that includes test-time inference and language-to-graph conversion to extract structured interactions from free-form text. Our findings highlight the limitations of current HOI detectors and the value of MLLMs for U-HOI. Code will be available at https://github.com/francescotonini/anyhoi

</details>


### [86] [Don't Let the Video Speak: Audio-Contrastive Preference Optimization for Audio-Visual Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14129)
*Ami Baid,Zihui Xue,Kristen Grauman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ACPO框架通过双轴偏好学习解决AVLM中的视频驱动音频幻觉问题，引入输出对比和输入对比目标来惩罚视觉主导的音频描述，提升音频真实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音频-视觉语言模型存在跨模态幻觉问题，特别是视频驱动的音频幻觉：模型经常利用视觉捷径来幻觉预期声音，而忽略真实的听觉证据。这种根深蒂固的视觉主导限制了模型的可靠性。

Method: 提出音频对比偏好优化（ACPO）框架，包含两个目标：1）输出对比目标惩罚伪装成音频事实的视觉描述；2）输入对比目标通过交换音频轨道来明确惩罚对真实听觉信号不变性的生成。

Result: 广泛实验表明，ACPO建立了高度忠实的音频基础，有效缓解了音频幻觉问题，同时不损害整体的多模态能力。

Conclusion: ACPO通过双轴偏好学习框架成功解决了AVLM中的视频驱动音频幻觉问题，提升了模型的音频真实性，为跨模态模型的可靠性提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While Audio-Visual Language Models (AVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress over recent years, their reliability is bottlenecked by cross-modal hallucination. A particularly pervasive manifestation is video-driven audio hallucination: models routinely exploit visual shortcuts to hallucinate expected sounds, discarding true auditory evidence. To counteract this deeply ingrained visual dominance, we propose Audio-Contrastive Preference Optimization (ACPO). This dual-axis preference learning framework introduces an output-contrastive objective to penalize visual descriptions masquerading as audio facts, alongside an input-contrastive objective that swaps audio tracks to explicitly penalize generation invariant to the true auditory signal. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACPO establishes highly faithful audio grounding and mitigates audio hallucination without compromising overarching multimodal capabilities.

</details>


### [87] [Geometric Context Transformer for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14141)
*Lin-Zhuo Chen,Jian Gao,Yihang Chen,Ka Leong Cheng,Yipengjing Sun,Liangxiao Hu,Nan Xue,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Yao Yao,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LingBot-Map是一个基于几何上下文变换器架构的前馈3D基础模型，用于从流数据重建场景，通过精心设计的注意力机制实现约20FPS的高效推理，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D重建需要几何精度、时间一致性和计算效率，受SLAM原理启发，需要解决坐标定位、密集几何线索和长距离漂移校正等问题。

Method: 基于几何上下文变换器(GCT)架构，设计包含锚点上下文、姿态参考窗口和轨迹记忆的注意力机制，保持流状态紧凑同时保留丰富几何上下文。

Result: 在518×378分辨率输入上实现约20FPS的稳定高效推理，支持超过10,000帧的长序列，在多个基准测试中优于现有流式和迭代优化方法。

Conclusion: LingBot-Map通过创新的注意力机制设计，成功解决了流式3D重建中的关键挑战，实现了高效、准确和稳定的场景重建。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction aims to recover 3D information, such as camera poses and point clouds, from a video stream, which necessitates geometric accuracy, temporal
  consistency, and computational efficiency. Motivated by the principles of Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM), we introduce LingBot-Map, a feed-forward 3D foundation
  model for reconstructing scenes from streaming data, built upon a geometric context transformer (GCT) architecture. A defining aspect of LingBot-Map lies in its carefully
  designed attention mechanism, which integrates an anchor context, a pose-reference window, and a trajectory memory to address coordinate grounding, dense geometric cues, and
  long-range drift correction, respectively. This design keeps the streaming state compact while retaining rich geometric context, enabling stable efficient inference at around
  20 FPS on 518 x 378 resolution inputs over long sequences exceeding 10,000 frames. Extensive evaluations across a variety of benchmarks demonstrate that our approach
  achieves superior performance compared to both existing streaming and iterative optimization-based approaches.

</details>


### [88] [SpatialEvo: Self-Evolving Spatial Intelligence via Deterministic Geometric Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14144)
*Dinging Li,Yingxiu Zhao,Xinrui Cheng,Kangheng Lin,Hongbo Peng,Hongxing Li,Zixuan Wang,Yuhong Dai,Haodong Li,Jia Wang,Yukang Shi,Liang Zhao,Jianjian Sun,Zheng Ge,Xiangyu Zhang,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialEvo：基于确定性几何环境的3D空间推理自演化框架，通过物理验证而非模型共识生成伪标签，实现无标注数据的持续改进


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D空间推理是具身智能的核心能力，但几何标注成本限制了模型持续改进。现有自演化范式依赖模型共识生成伪标签，会强化而非纠正模型的几何错误。作者发现3D空间推理的独特属性：真值可从点云和相机位姿精确计算，无需模型参与。

Method: 提出SpatialEvo框架，核心是确定性几何环境（DGE）。DGE将16个空间推理任务类别形式化为显式几何验证规则，将未标注3D场景转换为零噪声交互式预言机。单一共享参数策略在提问者和解答者角色下协同演化：提问者基于场景观察生成物理有效的空间问题，解答者针对DGE验证的真值得出精确答案。任务自适应调度器内生地集中训练模型最弱类别。

Result: 在9个基准测试中，SpatialEvo在3B和7B规模上均获得最高平均分数，在空间推理基准上取得一致增益，且在通用视觉理解上无性能下降。

Conclusion: 通过利用3D几何的确定性特性，SpatialEvo实现了无需人工标注的自演化空间推理，克服了传统自演化方法强化模型错误的局限，为具身智能的3D空间理解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning over three-dimensional scenes is a core capability for embodied intelligence, yet continuous model improvement remains bottlenecked by the cost of geometric annotation. The self-evolving paradigm offers a promising path, but its reliance on model consensus to construct pseudo-labels causes training to reinforce rather than correct the model's own geometric errors. We identify a property unique to 3D spatial reasoning that circumvents this limitation: ground truth is a deterministic consequence of the underlying geometry, computable exactly from point clouds and camera poses without any model involvement. Building on this insight, we present SpatialEvo, a self-evolving framework for 3D spatial reasoning, centered on the Deterministic Geometric Environment (DGE). The DGE formalizes 16 spatial reasoning task categories under explicit geometric validation rules and converts unannotated 3D scenes into zero-noise interactive oracles, replacing model consensus with objective physical feedback. A single shared-parameter policy co-evolves across questioner and solver roles under DGE constraints: the questioner generates physically valid spatial questions grounded in scene observations, while the solver derives precise answers against DGE-verified ground truth. A task-adaptive scheduler endogenously concentrates training on the model's weakest categories, producing a dynamic curriculum without manual design. Experiments across nine benchmarks demonstrate that SpatialEvo achieves the highest average score at both 3B and 7B scales, with consistent gains on spatial reasoning benchmarks and no degradation on general visual understanding.

</details>


### [89] [Seedance 2.0: Advancing Video Generation for World Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14148)
*Team Seedance,De Chen,Liyang Chen,Xin Chen,Ying Chen,Zhuo Chen,Zhuowei Chen,Feng Cheng,Tianheng Cheng,Yufeng Cheng,Mojie Chi,Xuyan Chi,Jian Cong,Qinpeng Cui,Fei Ding,Qide Dong,Yujiao Du,Haojie Duanmu,Junliang Fan,Jiarui Fang,Jing Fang,Zetao Fang,Chengjian Feng,Yu Gao,Diandian Gu,Dong Guo,Hanzhong Guo,Qiushan Guo,Boyang Hao,Hongxiang Hao,Haoxun He,Jiaao He,Qian He,Tuyen Hoang,Heng Hu,Ruoqing Hu,Yuxiang Hu,Jiancheng Huang,Weilin Huang,Zhaoyang Huang,Zhongyi Huang,Jishuo Jin,Ming Jing,Ashley Kim,Shanshan Lao,Yichong Leng,Bingchuan Li,Gen Li,Haifeng Li,Huixia Li,Jiashi Li,Ming Li,Xiaojie Li,Xingxing Li,Yameng Li,Yiying Li,Yu Li,Yueyan Li,Chao Liang,Han Liang,Jianzhong Liang,Ying Liang,Wang Liao,J. H. Lien,Shanchuan Lin,Xi Lin,Feng Ling,Yue Ling,Fangfang Liu,Jiawei Liu,Jihao Liu,Jingtuo Liu,Shu Liu,Sichao Liu,Wei Liu,Xue Liu,Zuxi Liu,Ruijie Lu,Lecheng Lyu,Jingting Ma,Tianxiang Ma,Xiaonan Nie,Jingzhe Ning,Junjie Pan,Xitong Pan,Ronggui Peng,Xueqiong Qu,Yuxi Ren,Yuchen Shen,Guang Shi,Lei Shi,Yinglong Song,Fan Sun,Li Sun,Renfei Sun,Wenjing Tang,Boyang Tao,Zirui Tao,Dongliang Wang,Feng Wang,Hulin Wang,Ke Wang,Qingyi Wang,Rui Wang,Shuai Wang,Shulei Wang,Weichen Wang,Xuanda Wang,Yanhui Wang,Yue Wang,Yuping Wang,Yuxuan Wang,Zijie Wang,Ziyu Wang,Guoqiang Wei,Meng Wei,Di Wu,Guohong Wu,Hanjie Wu,Huachao Wu,Jian Wu,Jie Wu,Ruolan Wu,Shaojin Wu,Xiaohu Wu,Xinglong Wu,Yonghui Wu,Ruiqi Xia,Xin Xia,Xuefeng Xiao,Shuang Xu,Bangbang Yang,Jiaqi Yang,Runkai Yang,Tao Yang,Yihang Yang,Zhixian Yang,Ziyan Yang,Fulong Ye,Bingqian Yi,Xing Yin,Yongbin You,Linxiao Yuan,Weihong Zeng,Xuejiao Zeng,Yan Zeng,Siyu Zhai,Zhonghua Zhai,Bowen Zhang,Chenlin Zhang,Heng Zhang,Jun Zhang,Manlin Zhang,Peiyuan Zhang,Shuo Zhang,Xiaohe Zhang,Xiaoying Zhang,Xinyan Zhang,Xinyi Zhang,Yichi Zhang,Zixiang Zhang,Haiyu Zhao,Huating Zhao,Liming Zhao,Yian Zhao,Guangcong Zheng,Jianbin Zheng,Xiaozheng Zheng,Zerong Zheng,Kuan Zhu,Feilong Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Seedance 2.0是中国2026年初发布的新一代原生多模态音视频生成模型，采用统一高效的大规模架构，支持文本、图像、音频、视频四种输入模态，具备行业最全面的多模态内容参考和编辑能力，在音视频生成各维度均有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发更先进的多模态音视频生成模型，超越前代Seedance 1.0和1.5 Pro的性能，提供更全面的多模态输入支持和更强大的内容生成能力，满足用户对高质量音视频内容创作的需求。

Method: 采用统一、高效、大规模的多模态音视频联合生成架构，支持四种输入模态（文本、图像、音频、视频），集成行业最全面的多模态内容参考和编辑能力，提供标准版和加速版（Seedance 2.0 Fast）两种变体。

Result: 在专家评估和公共用户测试中达到领域领先水平，支持4-15秒音视频内容直接生成，原生输出分辨率480p和720p，多模态输入支持最多3个视频片段、9张图像、3个音频片段，基础生成能力和多模态生成性能均有显著提升。

Conclusion: Seedance 2.0在多模态音视频生成领域实现了重大突破，提供了更全面的输入支持和更强大的生成能力，为用户带来了增强的创作体验，代表了该领域的技术进步。

Abstract: Seedance 2.0 is a new native multi-modal audio-video generation model, officially released in China in early February 2026. Compared with its predecessors, Seedance 1.0 and 1.5 Pro, Seedance 2.0 adopts a unified, highly efficient, and large-scale architecture for multi-modal audio-video joint generation. This allows it to support four input modalities: text, image, audio, and video, by integrating one of the most comprehensive suites of multi-modal content reference and editing capabilities available in the industry to date. It delivers substantial, well-rounded improvements across all key sub-dimensions of video and audio generation. In both expert evaluations and public user tests, the model has demonstrated performance on par with the leading levels in the field. Seedance 2.0 supports direct generation of audio-video content with durations ranging from 4 to 15 seconds, with native output resolutions of 480p and 720p. For multi-modal inputs as reference, its current open platform supports up to 3 video clips, 9 images, and 3 audio clips. In addition, we provide Seedance 2.0 Fast version, an accelerated variant of Seedance 2.0 designed to boost generation speed for low-latency scenarios. Seedance 2.0 has delivered significant improvements to its foundational generation capabilities and multi-modal generation performance, bringing an enhanced creative experience for end users.

</details>


### [90] [One Token per Highly Selective Frame: Towards Extreme Compression for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14149)
*Zheyu Zhang,Ziqi Pang,Shixing Chen,Xiang Hao,Vimal Bhat,Yu-Xiong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LP-Comp和QC-Comp两种压缩方法，实现长视频理解中的极端视频token压缩，达到每帧仅需一个token，显著提升模型处理长视频的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解对视觉语言模型具有挑战性，因为帧数多导致token数量庞大，受限于LLM的上下文长度，现有方法只能稀疏感知帧并丢失时间信息。

Method: 提出LP-Comp（可学习渐进式token级压缩）和QC-Comp（问题条件帧级压缩），结合局部注意力缓解位置偏差，实现token级和帧级双重压缩。

Result: 模型能处理2-4倍更多帧数，在LVBench上准确率从42.9%提升至46.2%，仅需2.5%的监督微调数据，在多个长视频基准测试中表现提升。

Conclusion: 通过可学习的token级压缩和问题条件的帧级压缩，实现了极端视频压缩，显著提升了长视频理解能力，且数据效率高。

Abstract: Long video understanding is inherently challenging for vision-language models (VLMs) because of the extensive number of frames. With each video frame typically expanding into tens or hundreds of tokens, the limited context length of large language models (LLMs) forces the VLMs to perceive the frames sparsely and lose temporal information. To address this, we explore extreme video token compression towards \emph{one token per frame} at the final LLM layer. Our key insight is that heuristic-based compression, widely adopted by previous methods, is prone to information loss, and this necessitates supervising LLM layers into \emph{learnable} and \emph{progressive} modules for \emph{token-level compression} (LP-Comp). Such compression enables our VLM to digest 2x-4x more frames with improved performance. To further increase the token efficiency, we investigate \emph{frame-level compression}, which selects the frames most relevant to the queries via the internal attention scores of the LLM layers, named \emph{question-conditioned compression} (QC-Comp). As a notable distinction from previous studies, we mitigate the position bias of LLM attention in long contexts, \emph{i.e.}, the over-concentration on the beginning and end of a sequence, by splitting long videos into short segments and employing local attention. Collectively, our combined \emph{token-level} and \emph{frame-level} leads to an e\textbf{x}treme compression model for long video understanding, named \textbf{\name}, achieving a significantly larger compression ratio and enabling denser frame sampling. Our \name is finetuned from VideoChat-Flash with a data-efficient \emph{supervised compression tuning} stage that only requires 2.5\% of the supervised fine-tuning data, yet boosts the accuracy from 42.9\% to 46.2\% on LVBench and enhances multiple other long video benchmarks.

</details>

### [91] [Robust Covert Quantum Communication under Bounded Channel Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13116)
*Abbas Arghavani,Alessandro V. Papadopoulos,Vahid Azimi Mousolou,Giuseppe Nebbione,Shahid Raza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究量子隐蔽通信在信道参数不确定条件下的鲁棒性保障，建立了考虑透射率和热噪声有界不确定性的复合量子光学信道模型，推导了适用于所有允许信道实现的隐蔽性和可靠性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实光学链路（卫星、光纤、自由空间系统）中，信道参数（透射率、背景噪声）会因环境波动、校准噪声和估计误差而变化。传统量子隐蔽通信分析基于理想假设，即这些参数完全已知且恒定，无法应对实际系统中的参数不确定性。

Method: 开发了在不确定性条件下认证隐蔽性和可靠性的鲁棒框架，考虑透射率和热噪声有界不确定性的复合量子光学信道。推导了最坏情况下可可靠传输的隐蔽量子比特数的闭式下界，通过QuTiP模拟四模玻色模型验证隐蔽性项，并结合分析可靠性界评估鲁棒有效载荷。

Result: 发现鲁棒性不能简单地将最坏情况参数值插入已知信道界中获得，因为对隐蔽性和可靠性最不利的信道实现通常出现在不确定性集的不同角落，这产生了安全系统设计的基本权衡。识别了超过该界限保证有效载荷降至零的尖锐可行性边界，并量化了不确定性导致的安全惩罚。

Conclusion: 该研究将量子隐蔽通信从名义上的完美知识分析推进到不确定性条件下经认证的最坏情况操作，为实际量子通信系统设计提供了鲁棒性保障框架。

Abstract: Covert quantum communication is usually analyzed under idealized assumptions that channel parameters, such as transmissivity and background noise, are perfectly known and constant. In realistic optical links, including satellite, fiber, and free-space systems, these parameters vary because of environmental fluctuations, calibration noise, and estimation errors. We study covert quantum communication over compound quantum optical channels with bounded uncertainty in both transmissivity and thermal noise, and derive guarantees that hold for all admissible channel realizations.
  We develop a robust framework for certifying both covertness and reliability under uncertainty. A central finding is that robustness cannot be obtained by simply inserting worst-case parameter values into known-channel bounds: the channel realizations that are most adverse for covertness and reliability generally occur at different corners of the uncertainty set. This creates a fundamental trade-off in secure system design.
  We derive a closed-form lower bound on the worst-case guaranteed number of covert qubits that can be transmitted reliably, identify a sharp feasibility boundary beyond which the guaranteed payload drops to zero, and quantify the security penalty caused by uncertainty. We validate the covertness term with QuTiP simulations of a four-mode bosonic model and combine it with an analytical reliability bound to evaluate the robust payload. Our results move covert quantum communication from nominal perfect-knowledge analysis to certified worst-case operation under uncertainty.

</details>


### [92] [Conflict-Aware Robust Design for Covert Wireless Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13122)
*Abbas Arghavani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文研究无线隐蔽通信中可靠性与隐蔽性在有限不确定性下的冲突关系，发现两者受不同最坏情况条件支配，提出冲突感知的鲁棒设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线隐蔽通信需要在可靠性与隐蔽性之间取得平衡，但不确定性既有助于隐藏信号又使系统设计复杂化。核心问题是：在有限不确定性下，可靠性与隐蔽性是否受相同的最坏条件支配？如果不是，鲁棒隐蔽设计就不能简化为单一的最坏情况环境。

Method: 采用具有准静态衰落的隐蔽无线模型，Bob端基于中断的可靠性约束，Willie端采用辐射计检测。不确定性通过Bob平均信道强度和Willie噪声功率的有界区间表示。为获得可处理的特征，采用条件大N中点阈值替代Willie检测器，参数化为Willie侧衰落实现。

Result: 研究表明：可靠性约束由Bob的最小可接受信道参数支配，而隐蔽性约束由Willie的最小可接受噪声水平支配。这确立了冲突感知的鲁棒设计原则：可靠性与隐蔽性的不利实现是不同的。基于此推导了鲁棒可行发射功率和相应鲁棒最优速率的闭式表达式。

Conclusion: 即使在简化的无线设置中，鲁棒隐蔽设计也需要对可靠性和隐蔽性采用不同的最坏情况推理。数值结果表明，有限不确定性会收缩可行区域，单调降低鲁棒最优速率，并可能导致相对于名义设计的显著损失。

Abstract: Covert wireless communication aims to establish a reliable link while hiding the transmission from an adversary. In wireless settings, uncertainty plays a central role in this tradeoff: it can help mask the signal from a warden, but it also complicates robust system design. This raises a basic question: under bounded uncertainty, are reliability and covertness governed by the same adverse conditions? If not, robust covert design cannot be reduced to a single worst-case environment. In this paper, we study this question in a covert wireless model with quasi-static fading, outage-based reliability at Bob and radiometric detection at Willie. Uncertainty is represented through bounded intervals for Bob's average channel strength and Willie's noise power. To obtain a tractable characterization, we adopt a conditional large-N midpoint-threshold surrogate for Willie's detector, parameterized by a Willie-side fading realization. Within this framework, we show that the reliability constraint is governed by Bob's smallest admissible channel parameter, whereas the covertness constraint is governed by Willie's smallest admissible noise level. This establishes a conflict-aware robust-design principle: the adverse realizations for reliability and covertness differ. Based on this result, we derive closed-form expressions for the robustly feasible transmit power and the corresponding robust optimal rate. Numerical results show that bounded uncertainty contracts the feasible region, monotonically reduces the robust optimal rate, and can cause substantial loss relative to the nominal design. Monte Carlo results further show that the conditional surrogate closely tracks the midpoint-threshold radiometer in the intended low-effective-SNR regime. Overall, the paper shows that even in a streamlined wireless setting, robust covert design requires different adverse-case reasoning for reliability and covertness.

</details>


### [93] [Honeypot Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13301)
*Najmul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"蜜罐协议"来检测AI模型的情境依赖行为，通过在不同监控条件下测试模型表现，发现Claude Opus 4.6在所有条件下都保持100%主任务成功率和零侧任务触发


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统可信监控作为AI控制的标准防御存在漏洞：易受自适应攻击、共谋和策略性攻击选择的影响。这些漏洞利用了监控的被动性——只观察模型行为而不探测模型在不同感知条件下是否会表现不同。

Method: 提出蜜罐协议，通过仅改变系统提示来测试情境依赖行为，在三个条件下进行测试：评估条件、合成部署条件、明确无监控条件。保持任务、环境和评分完全一致。在BashArena环境中评估Claude Opus 4.6模型，在诚实模式和攻击模式下测试所有三个条件。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6在所有三个条件下都实现了100%主任务成功率和零侧任务触发，表现一致。这为未来与更强攻击策略和更多模型的比较提供了基准。

Conclusion: 蜜罐协议能够有效检测AI模型的情境依赖行为，为AI安全监控提供了更主动的测试方法。当前测试模型在所有条件下表现一致，为未来研究建立了基准。

Abstract: Trusted monitoring, the standard defense in AI control, is vulnerable to adaptive attacks, collusion, and strategic attack selection. All of these exploit the fact that monitoring is passive: it observes model behavior but never probes whether the model would behave differently under different perceived conditions. We introduce the honeypot protocol, which tests for context-dependent behavior by varying only the system prompt across three conditions (evaluation, synthetic deployment, explicit no-monitoring) while holding the task, environment, and scoring identical. We evaluate Claude Opus 4.6 in BashArena across all three conditions in both honest and attack modes. The model achieved 100% main task success and triggered zero side tasks uniformly across conditions, providing a baseline for future comparisons with stronger attack policies and additional models.

</details>


### [94] [Secure and Privacy-Preserving Vertical Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13474)
*Shan Jin,Sai Rahul Rachuri,Yizhen Wang,Anderson C. A. Nascimento,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个端到端的隐私保护联邦学习框架，针对垂直分割场景，通过多服务器MPC协议和差分隐私技术保护输入输出隐私


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决垂直分割联邦学习中的隐私保护问题，特别是在特征分布在客户端且标签不共享的场景下，需要同时保护输入和输出隐私

Method: 将聚合器角色分布到多个服务器，运行安全多方计算协议进行模型和特征聚合，应用差分隐私到最终发布的模型；优化方案减少MPC计算和通信开销

Result: 实验结果显示协议的有效性，相比朴素方案大幅减少了多方计算的计算和通信量

Conclusion: 提出的框架为垂直分割联邦学习提供了实用的隐私保护解决方案，通过MPC和DP的组合实现了端到端隐私保护

Abstract: We propose a novel end-to-end privacy-preserving framework, instantiated by three efficient protocols for different deployment scenarios, covering both input and output privacy, for the vertically split scenario in federated learning (FL), where features are split across clients and labels are not shared by all parties. We do so by distributing the role of the aggregator in FL into multiple servers and having them run secure multiparty computation (MPC) protocols to perform model and feature aggregation and apply differential privacy (DP) to the final released model. While a naive solution would have the clients delegating the entirety of training to run in MPC between the servers, our optimized solution, which supports purely global and also global-local models updates with privacy-preserving, drastically reduces the amount of computation and communication performed using multiparty computation. The experimental results also show the effectiveness of our protocols.

</details>


### [95] [Where Trust Fails: Mapping Location-Data Provenance Risks in Europe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13668)
*Eduardo Brito,Liina Kamm*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将欧洲数字主权和安全问题框架化为位置数据溯源问题，提出将位置视为数字原语，并引入证明位置机制作为可验证存在声明的候选能力层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧洲数字主权和安全日益依赖于能否在对抗压力下基于可信的位置证据做出高影响力决策。当前存在不对称性：位置、存在、路由或管辖权的声明成本低廉，而机构和受影响方在争议发生时却面临昂贵的重建成本。

Method: 论文引入紧凑的风险分类法，将溯源失败分解为完整性轴和重复出现的故障模式，并推导出以争议下的可争议性为中心、同时保持隐私和权利兼容性的下一代数字信任基础设施的设计期望。

Result: 提出了将位置视为数字原语的概念，应表示为证据承载的声明而非自我声明的坐标，并将证明位置机制定位为在明确威胁和隐私假设下产生可验证存在声明的候选能力层。

Conclusion: 为欧洲下一代数字信任基础设施的未来架构工作提供了跨部门中立的基础，强调在争议下的可争议性、隐私和权利兼容性，以解决位置数据溯源的核心挑战。

Abstract: European digital sovereignty and security increasingly depends on whether high-impact decisions can be grounded in location evidence that remains credible under adversarial pressure. This paper frames a cross-sector analysis as a location-data provenance problem: not merely what a device or service reports as location, but whether there is contestable evidence about where and when an asserted event occurred, who or what produced the assertion, and under which audit and retention guarantees. There are observable patterns across democratic processes and the information environment, trade and origin-sensitive supply chains, finance and illicit shipping flows, critical infrastructure and mobility, and harms targeting individuals' private and social domains. In these patterns we see a recurring asymmetry in which locality, presence, routing, or jurisdiction can be asserted cheaply while institutions and affected parties face costly reconstruction when disputes arise. To make this challenge actionable, this paper introduces a compact risk taxonomy that decomposes provenance failures into integrity axes and recurring failure modes, and derives design expectations for next-generation digital trust infrastructure centered on contestability under dispute, while remaining privacy- and rights-compatible. It argues for treating location as a digital primitive that should be represented as evidence-bearing claims rather than self-asserted coordinates, and positions proof-of-location (PoL) mechanisms as a candidate capability layer for producing verifiable presence claims under explicit threat and privacy assumptions. The outcome is a sector-neutral foundation for future architectural work on a next-generation digital trust infrastructure for Europe.

</details>


### [96] [RealVuln: Benchmarking Rule-Based, General-Purpose LLM, and Security-Specialized Scanners on Real-World Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13764)
*John Pellew,Faizan Raza*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RealVuln是首个开源基准测试，比较了基于规则的SAST、通用LLM和安全专用扫描器在26个故意存在漏洞的Python仓库上的表现，结果显示安全专用扫描器Kolega.Dev在F3分数上领先，通用LLM Claude Sonnet 4.6次之，基于规则的Semgrep最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全扫描器在真实代码上的性能评估缺乏系统比较，需要建立开放基准来评估基于规则的SAST、通用LLM和安全专用扫描器的实际效果，为开发者和安全团队提供参考。

Method: 创建包含26个故意存在漏洞的Python仓库（教育和CTF应用）的数据集，包含796个手工标注条目（676个漏洞，120个误报陷阱）。测试15个扫描器（3个基于规则的SAST，10个通用LLM，2个安全专用），使用F3分数（beta=3，召回率权重是精确度的9倍）进行排名。

Result: 在所有指标下都出现了清晰的三层排名结构。在F3分数下，安全专用扫描器Kolega.Dev（73.0）领先，其次是通用LLM Claude Sonnet 4.6（51.7），而最好的基于规则工具Semgrep（17.7）得分仅为前者的约1/3。在F1分数下，Sonnet 4.6领先（60.9），Kolega.Dev为52.4。

Conclusion: 安全专用扫描器在漏洞检测方面表现最佳，通用LLM也显示出强大潜力，而传统基于规则的SAST工具效果相对较差。RealVuln作为持续发展的基准测试，将向多语言覆盖方向发展，所有代码和数据均已开源。

Abstract: How do security scanners perform on real-world code? We present RealVuln, the first open-source benchmark comparing Rule-Based SAST, General-Purpose LLMs, and Security-Specialized scanners on 26 intentionally vulnerable Python repositories (educational and Capture-The-Flag applications) with 796 hand-labeled entries (676 vulnerabilities, 120 false-positive traps). We test 15 scanners (3 Rule-Based SAST, 10 General-Purpose LLM, 2 Security-Specialized) and rank them by F3 score (beta=3, weighting recall 9x over precision). A clear three-tier ranking emerges under all metrics. Under F3, the Security-Specialized scanner Kolega.Dev (73.0) leads, followed by the best General-Purpose LLM, Claude Sonnet 4.6 (51.7), which in turn scores nearly 3x higher than the best Rule-Based tool, Semgrep (17.7). Under F1, Sonnet 4.6 leads (60.9) with Kolega.Dev at 52.4. Rankings within tiers shift with beta, but the three-tier hierarchy holds across all weightings. All code, ground-truth data, scanner outputs, and scoring scripts are released under an open-source license. An interactive dashboard is at https://realvuln.kolega.dev/. RealVuln is a living benchmark: versioned, community-driven, with a roadmap toward multi-language coverage.

</details>


### [97] [MCPThreatHive: Automated Threat Intelligence for Model Context Protocol Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13849)
*Yi Ting Shen,Kentaroh Toyoda,Alex Leung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MCPThreatHive是一个开源平台，用于自动化MCP威胁情报的全生命周期管理，填补现有MCP安全工具在组合攻击建模、持续威胁情报和统一多框架分类方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的智能体系统快速发展带来了新的安全威胁类别，现有安全框架无法充分应对这些威胁，需要专门的解决方案。

Method: 开发MCPThreatHive平台，实现端到端的MCP威胁情报生命周期自动化：包括多源数据收集、AI驱动的威胁提取与分类、结构化知识图谱存储和交互式可视化。平台采用MCP-38威胁分类法，将38种MCP特定威胁模式映射到STRIDE、OWASP LLM应用Top 10和OWASP智能体应用Top 10框架，并使用复合风险评分模型进行量化优先级排序。

Result: 通过对比现有代表性MCP安全工具，识别出三个关键覆盖空白：不完整的组合攻击建模、缺乏持续威胁情报、缺少统一的多框架分类。MCPThreatHive平台成功填补了这些空白。

Conclusion: MCPThreatHive提供了一个全面的开源解决方案，专门针对MCP智能体系统的安全威胁，通过自动化威胁情报生命周期和标准化分类框架，显著提升了MCP系统的安全防护能力。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Model Context Protocol (MCP)-based agentic systems has introduced a new category of security threats that existing frameworks are inadequately equipped to address. We present MCPThreatHive, an open-source platform that automates the end-to-end lifecycle of MCP threat intelligence: from continuous, multi-source data collection through AI-driven threat extraction and classification, to structured knowledge graph storage and interactive visualization. The platform operationalizes the MCP-38 threat taxonomy, a curated set of 38 MCP-specific threat patterns mapped to STRIDE, OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications, and OWASP Top 10 for Agentic Applications. A composite risk scoring model provides quantitative prioritization. Through a comparative analysis of representative existing MCP security tools, we identify three critical coverage gaps that MCPThreatHive addresses: incomplete compositional attack modeling, absence of continuous threat intelligence, and lack of unified multi-framework classification.

</details>


### [98] [KindHML: formal verification of smart contracts based on Hennessy-Milner logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14038)
*Massimo Bartoletti,Angelo Ferrando,Enrico Lipparini,Vadim Malvone*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于形式化验证的智能合约安全分析方法，能够检测跨多笔交易的复杂时序攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能合约管理大量资产，其安全性至关重要。现实世界中的攻击（如流动性攻击、抢先交易攻击）通常利用跨多笔交易的业务逻辑缺陷，而现有分析工具无法有效检测这些需要时序推理的攻击。

Method: 提出了一种自动化方法，将Solidity合约的特定子集和基于一阶Hennessy-Milner逻辑的时序规范编码为Lustre规范语言，然后利用Kind 2模型检查器验证合约是否符合规范。

Result: 实现了一个集成翻译和验证步骤的工具链，在包含复杂攻击场景的智能合约和时序属性基准测试中评估了其有效性和性能。结果表明，该方法能够有效验证智能合约的非平凡时序属性，并检测出现有分析工具无法发现的违规行为。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的形式化验证方法能够处理复杂的时序属性，填补了现有智能合约分析工具在检测跨交易攻击方面的能力空白，为智能合约安全提供了更强大的验证手段。

Abstract: Smart contracts deployed on blockchains such as Ethereum routinely manage large amounts of assets, making their security critical. Empirical studies show that real-world attacks often exploit flaws in the business logic of contracts that unfold across multiple transactions, such as liquidity or front-running attacks. Detecting these attacks requires reasoning about expressive temporal properties beyond the capabilities of existing analysis tools. In this paper, we present an automated approach to the formal verification of smart contracts, enabling the specification and verification of complex temporal properties. Our approach provides a fully automated encoding into Lustre -- the specification language supported by the Kind 2 model checker -- of an expressive subset of Solidity contracts and temporal specifications based on first-order Hennessy-Milner Logic. This encoding allows us to leverage Kind 2 to determine whether the contract respects the specification or not. We implement our approach in a toolchain that integrates the translation and verification steps, and we evaluate its effectiveness and performance on a benchmark of smart contracts and temporal properties capturing complex attack scenarios. Our results show that the proposed approach can effectively verify non-trivial temporal properties of smart contracts and detect violations that are beyond the reach of existing analysis tools.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [Rare Event Analysis via Stochastic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13213)
*Yuanqi Du,Jiajun He,Dinghuai Zhang,Eric Vanden-Eijnden,Carles Domingo-Enrich*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 将过渡路径理论中的承诺函数估计转化为随机最优控制问题，通过反馈控制主动引导轨迹进入反应区域，实现反应路径的高效采样


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子构象变化、相变和化学反应等罕见事件在物理系统中至关重要，但无偏模拟很难产生这些事件。过渡路径理论提供了分析这些事件的严格统计框架，但其核心承诺函数的准确估计具有挑战性

Method: 将承诺函数估计转化为随机最优控制问题，承诺函数定义了与对数梯度成正比的反馈控制。开发了两种互补的目标函数：直接反向传播损失和基于原理的非策略值匹配损失。针对亚稳态问题，引入了替代采样过程，在保持反应电流的同时降低有效能垒

Result: 在基准系统上，该方法比现有方法获得了显著更准确的承诺函数估计、反应速率和平衡常数

Conclusion: 随机最优控制框架为罕见事件采样提供了新的有效方法，通过主动控制机制克服了传统方法的采样困难，在物理系统罕见事件研究中具有重要应用价值

Abstract: Rare events such as conformational changes in biomolecules, phase transitions, and chemical reactions are central to the behavior of many physical systems, yet they are extremely difficult to study computationally because unbiased simulations seldom produce them. Transition Path Theory (TPT) provides a rigorous statistical framework for analyzing such events: it characterizes the ensemble of reactive trajectories between two designated metastable states (reactant and product), and its central object--the committor function, which gives the probability that the system will next reach the product rather than the reactant--encodes all essential kinetic and thermodynamic information. We introduce a framework that casts committor estimation as a stochastic optimal control (SOC) problem. In this formulation the committor defines a feedback control--proportional to the gradient of its logarithm--that actively steers trajectories toward the reactive region, thereby enabling efficient sampling of reactive paths. To solve the resulting hitting-time control problem we develop two complementary objectives: a direct backpropagation loss and a principled off-policy Value Matching loss, for which we establish first-order optimality guarantees. We further address metastability, which can trap controlled trajectories in intermediate basins, by introducing an alternative sampling process that preserves the reactive current while lowering effective energy barriers. On benchmark systems, the framework yields markedly more accurate committor estimates, reaction rates, and equilibrium constants than existing methods.

</details>


### [100] [Identifiability of Potentially Degenerate Gaussian Mixture Models With Piecewise Affine Mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13218)
*Danru Xu,Sébastien Lachapelle,Sara Magliacane*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究因果表示学习中的可识别性问题，针对潜在变量服从可能退化的高斯混合分布且通过分段仿射混合函数观测的情况，提出基于稀疏正则化的可识别性理论及两阶段估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果表示学习旨在从高维观测中识别潜在变量，但当变量相互依赖且观测函数为分段仿射时，特别是当潜在变量服从可能退化的高斯混合分布时，概率密度函数定义不明确，这给可识别性带来了挑战。

Method: 提出基于稀疏正则化的可识别性理论框架，并设计两阶段估计方法：第一阶段通过稀疏正则化学习表示，第二阶段通过高斯性约束进一步优化，以恢复潜在变量。

Result: 理论分析提供了一系列逐步增强的可识别性结果，实验验证了方法在合成数据和图像数据上有效恢复真实潜在变量的能力。

Conclusion: 该研究为退化高斯混合分布和分段仿射混合函数下的因果表示学习提供了可识别性理论保证和实用估计方法，解决了概率密度函数定义不明确带来的挑战。

Abstract: Causal representation learning (CRL) aims to identify the underlying latent variables from high-dimensional observations, even when variables are dependent with each other. We study this problem for latent variables that follow a potentially degenerate Gaussian mixture distribution and that are only observed through the transformation via a piecewise affine mixing function. We provide a series of progressively stronger identifiability results for this challenging setting in which the probability density functions are ill-defined because of the potential degeneracy. For identifiability up to permutation and scaling, we leverage a sparsity regularization on the learned representation. Based on our theoretical results, we propose a two-stage method to estimate the latent variables by enforcing sparsity and Gaussianity in the learned representations. Experiments on synthetic and image data highlight our method's effectiveness in recovering the ground-truth latent variables.

</details>


### [101] [Robust Low-Rank Tensor Completion based on M-product with Weighted Correlated Total Variation and Sparse Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13525)
*Biswarup Karmakar,Ratikanta Behera*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出TWCTV正则化方法，通过加权Schatten-p范数和稀疏分量，在M-乘积框架下解决张量补全问题，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有张量补全方法依赖均匀正则化方案（张量核范数和ℓ1范数），对所有奇异值和稀疏分量施加相同收缩，破坏了关键张量结构，需要更精细的正则化方法。

Method: 提出张量加权相关全变分(TWCTV)正则化器，结合梯度张量的加权Schatten-p范数（低秩性）、平滑性约束和加权稀疏分量（噪声抑制），采用自适应加权方案降低阈值水平，保护主导奇异值和稀疏分量。

Result: 通过增强的ADMM算法实现计算效率和理论保证，在图像补全、去噪和背景减除任务中验证了相对于基准方法的优越性能。

Conclusion: TWCTV方法通过自适应加权方案有效保护张量结构细节，在M-乘积框架下实现了更精确的张量恢复，为鲁棒低秩张量补全提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The robust low-rank tensor completion problem addresses the challenge of recovering corrupted high-dimensional tensor data with missing entries, outliers, and sparse noise commonly found in real-world applications. Existing methodologies have encountered fundamental limitations due to their reliance on uniform regularization schemes, particularly the tensor nuclear norm and $\ell_1$ norm regularization approaches, which indiscriminately apply equal shrinkage to all singular values and sparse components, thereby compromising the preservation of critical tensor structures. The proposed tensor weighted correlated total variation (TWCTV) regularizer addresses these shortcomings through an $M$-product framework that combines a weighted Schatten-$p$ norm on gradient tensors for low-rankness with smoothness enforcement and weighted sparse components for noise suppression. The proposed weighting scheme adaptively reduces the thresholding level to preserve both dominant singular values and sparse components, thus improving the reconstruction of critical structural elements and nuanced details in the recovered signal. Through a systematic algorithmic approach, we introduce an enhanced alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) that offers both computational efficiency and theoretical substantiation, with convergence properties comprehensively analyzed within the $M$-product framework.Comprehensive numerical evaluations across image completion, denoising, and background subtraction tasks validate the superior performance of this approach relative to established benchmark methods.

</details>


### [102] [Covariance-adapting algorithm for semi-bandits with application to sparse rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13738)
*Pierre Perrault,Vianney Perchet,Michal Valko*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文研究随机组合半赌博机问题，提出基于协方差矩阵而非传统子高斯假设的新下界，并设计了使用协方差估计的算法，最后将结果扩展到稀疏结果的应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统随机组合半赌博机问题通常假设结果服从特定参数分布（如子高斯分布），这些参数在实践中难以估计。作者旨在缓解这一问题，考虑更一般的子指数分布族，该族包含有界分布和高斯分布，从而降低对先验知识的依赖。

Method: 1. 提出新的子指数分布族，包含有界和高斯分布；2. 证明基于未知结果协方差矩阵的期望遗憾下界，该下界比子高斯矩阵更紧；3. 构建使用协方差估计的算法；4. 对算法进行严格的渐近遗憾分析；5. 将结果应用于稀疏结果族，适用于推荐系统等应用场景。

Result: 1. 建立了基于协方差矩阵的新下界，比传统子高斯假设更紧；2. 设计了使用协方差估计的算法；3. 提供了紧的渐近遗憾分析；4. 成功将理论扩展到稀疏结果场景，为推荐系统等应用提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文通过考虑更一般的子指数分布族，解决了传统随机组合半赌博机中对特定参数分布先验知识的依赖问题。提出的基于协方差矩阵的下界更紧，算法设计更实用，理论结果可扩展到稀疏结果场景，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: We investigate stochastic combinatorial semi-bandits, where the entire joint distribution of outcomes impacts the complexity of the problem instance (unlike in the standard bandits). Typical distributions considered depend on specific parameter values, whose prior knowledge is required in theory but quite difficult to estimate in practice; an example is the commonly assumed sub-Gaussian family. We alleviate this issue by instead considering a new general family of sub-exponential distributions, which contains bounded and Gaussian ones. We prove a new lower bound on the expected regret on this family, that is parameterized by the unknown covariance matrix of outcomes, a tighter quantity than the sub-Gaussian matrix. We then construct an algorithm that uses covariance estimates, and provide a tight asymptotic analysis of the regret. Finally, we apply and extend our results to the family of sparse outcomes, which has applications in many recommender systems.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [The Integer-valued Moving-Average Random Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13779)
*Angelika Silbernagel,Christian H. Weiß*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出并研究了一种用于计数随机场的整数值移动平均（INMA）模型，推导了任意阶数下的边际分布和空间依赖结构的闭式表达式，展示了该模型可配备泊松边际分布及多种可解释的依赖结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为计数随机场开发一种具有闭式表达式的整数值移动平均模型，以便能够精确分析其统计特性，特别是边际分布和空间依赖结构。

Method: 提出整数值移动平均（INMA）模型，推导任意阶数下的闭式表达式，包括边际分布、二元分布和自协方差函数，并展示模型可配备泊松边际分布。

Result: 成功推导了INMA模型的闭式表达式，证明了模型可配备泊松边际分布，并展示了多种可解释的空间依赖结构。

Conclusion: INMA模型为计数随机场提供了一种灵活的分析框架，具有闭式表达式和多种可解释的依赖结构，适用于空间计数数据分析。

Abstract: An integer-valued moving average (INMA) model for count random fields is proposed and investigated. Closed-form expressions are derived for both its marginal distribution and spatial dependence structure, for arbitrary model order. In particular, general expressions for bivariate distributions and autocovariances are provided. It is shown that the INMA random field can be equipped (among others) with a Poisson marginal distribution. It is also illustrated that different and well-interpretable dependence structures are possible.

</details>


### [104] [Finite-Step Bounds for Iterated Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14071)
*Ishrak AlhajjHassan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文为Pearson相关矩阵迭代动态建立了有限步概率上界框架，量化了收缩比ρ_k的扩张行为，并通过经验条件分位数方法构建了可靠的边界函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pearson相关矩阵迭代动态中，虽然Δ_k趋于零，但收缩比ρ_k可能在有限步内超过1，这种有限步扩张行为无法通过局部线性化分析捕捉，需要建立概率上界框架来描述这些现象。

Method: 初始化P_0为独立同分布的[-1,1]均匀分布矩阵，定义Δ_k和ρ_k。通过经验条件p-分位数方法构建状态依赖边界函数B_p(δ_k)，使用对数分箱技术估计给定δ_k下log ρ_k的条件分布，并通过乘性调整得到更保守的边界族。

Result: 验证显示边界在n∈[3,2000]范围内保持经验覆盖率与名义水平匹配。具体结果：δ≤0.03时ρ≤1的概率≥0.95（与n无关）；95%置信度下ρ≤1.7在21/22维度成立；n=69时发现极端上尾不连续性（ρ≤2.35）。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为Pearson相关动态建立了有限步概率边界框架，揭示了局部线性化无法捕捉的有限步扩张现象，提供了可重现的代码和数据，为相关矩阵迭代分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We establish finite-step probabilistic upper bounds on the contraction ratios $ρ_k = Δ_{k+1}/Δ_k$ for iterated Pearson correlation dynamics. Let $(P_k)_{k\ge 0}$ be the sequence generated by the Pearson update. Define $Δ_k := \|P_{k+1}-P_k\|_F$, $ρ_k := Δ_{k+1}/Δ_k$ for $Δ_k > 0$, and $δ_k := Δ_k/n$. Although $Δ_k \to 0$ along convergent trajectories, the ratios $ρ_k$ may exceed unity in finitely many steps. This behavior is invisible to local linearization. Our main contribution is a probabilistic bounding framework that captures these finite-step expansions.
  We initialize $P_0$ with i.i.d. $\mathcal{U}[-1,1]$ entries and let $\mathbb{P}$ be the induced measure. For $k \ge 2$, we construct state-dependent bounds $B_p : \mathbb{R}_+ \to \mathbb{R}_+$ satisfying $\mathbb{P}(ρ_k \le B_p(δ_k)) \ge p$. The functions $B^{\mathrm{q}}_p(δ)$ are empirical conditional $p$-quantiles of $\log ρ_k$ given $δ_k$ under logarithmic binning. Larger families $B^{\mathrm{TC}}_{p,τ}(δ)$ and $B^{\mathrm{tol}}_{p,τ}(δ)$ are obtained via multiplicative adjustments, yielding pointwise larger bounds that preserve the $δ$-dependence.
  Validation on held-out trajectories confirms the bounds hold with empirical coverage matching nominal levels for all $n \in [3,2000]$. The baseline $0.95$-quantile bound $B^{\mathrm{q}}_{0.95}(δ)$ yields two concrete results: $\mathbb{P}(ρ\le 1 \mid δ\le 0.03) \ge 0.95$ uniformly in $n$, and $\mathbb{P}(ρ\le 1.7) \ge 0.95$ for 21 of 22 dimensions. The exception $n = 69$ attains $2.35$, revealing a rare extreme upper tail discontinuity not captured by asymptotic analysis. These are the first finite-step probabilistic bounds for Pearson correlation dynamics. The framework is fully reproducible with provided code and data.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [105] [Bias-Corrected Adaptive Conformal Inference for Multi-Horizon Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13253)
*Ankit Lade,Sai Krishna J.,Indar Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BC-ACI在标准ACI基础上加入偏差校正，通过在线EWMA估计预测偏差并重新调整置信区间中心，解决分布漂移下的区间过宽问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ACI只能调整分位数阈值而不能移动区间中心，当基础预测器在分布漂移后出现持续偏差时，ACI通过对称地加宽区间来补偿，导致不必要的保守区间。需要一种能直接校正偏差根源而非症状的方法。

Method: 提出偏差校正ACI（BC-ACI），在标准ACI基础上增加在线指数加权移动平均（EWMA）来估计预测偏差。该方法在分位数计算前校正非一致性分数，并重新调整预测区间中心。采用自适应死区阈值抑制噪声级别的偏差校正，确保在数据校准良好时性能不下降。

Result: 在688次实验（涵盖两个基础模型、四种合成分布漂移和三个真实数据集）中，BC-ACI在均值和复合分布漂移下将Winkler区间分数降低了13-17%（Wilcoxon p < 0.001），在平稳数据上保持等效性能（比率1.002x）。有限样本分析显示覆盖保证随偏差估计误差适度下降。

Conclusion: BC-ACI通过直接校正预测偏差而非仅调整区间宽度，显著提高了自适应共形推断在分布漂移下的效率，同时保持了覆盖保证。该方法解决了ACI在应对预测器持续偏差时的根本局限性。

Abstract: Adaptive Conformal Inference (ACI) provides distribution-free prediction intervals with asymptotic coverage guarantees for time series under distribution shift. However, ACI only adapts the quantile threshold -- it cannot shift the interval center. When a base forecaster develops persistent bias after a regime change, ACI compensates by widening intervals symmetrically, producing unnecessarily conservative bands. We propose Bias-Corrected ACI (BC-ACI), which augments standard ACI with an online exponentially weighted moving average (EWM) estimate of forecast bias. BC-ACI corrects nonconformity scores before quantile computation and re-centers prediction intervals, addressing the root cause of miscalibration rather than its symptom. An adaptive dead-zone threshold suppresses corrections when estimated bias is indistinguishable from noise, ensuring no degradation on well-calibrated data. In controlled experiments across 688 runs spanning two base models, four synthetic regimes, and three real datasets, BC-ACI reduces Winkler interval scores by 13--17% under mean and compound distribution shifts (Wilcoxon p < 0.001) while maintaining equivalent performance on stationary data (ratio 1.002x). We provide finite-sample analysis showing that coverage guarantees degrade gracefully with bias estimation error.

</details>


### [106] [Some Theoretical Limitations of t-SNE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13295)
*Rupert Li,Elchanan Mossel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析了t-SNE降维技术可能导致重要数据特征丢失的问题，并建立了数学框架来理解这种损失


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE作为流行的降维和可视化技术，虽然广泛应用，但已知所有降维技术都可能丢失数据的重要特征。作者旨在建立数学框架来系统理解t-SNE在何种情况下会丢失数据的重要特征

Method: 建立数学框架，通过在不同场景下证明一系列结果，展示t-SNE如何丢失数据的重要特征

Result: 提供了多个场景下的数学结果，具体展示了t-SNE在降维过程中丢失重要数据特征的方式和机制

Conclusion: t-SNE降维确实会丢失数据的重要特征，作者建立的数学框架为理解这种损失提供了理论基础，有助于更谨慎地使用t-SNE并解释其结果

Abstract: t-SNE has gained popularity as a dimension reduction technique, especially for visualizing data. It is well-known that all dimension reduction techniques may lose important features of the data. We provide a mathematical framework for understanding this loss for t-SNE by establishing a number of results in different scenarios showing how important features of data are lost by using t-SNE.

</details>


### [107] [Spectral Thompson sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13739)
*Tomas Kocak,Michal Valko,Remi Munos,Shipra Agrawal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SpectralTS算法：基于图结构的Thompson Sampling变体，用于解决图平滑多臂老虎机问题，计算效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Thompson Sampling在计算广告等领域表现出色但理论分析工具较新；传统算法在处理图结构选择问题时扩展性差；需要开发能利用图结构平滑性且计算高效的算法。

Method: 提出SpectralTS算法，利用图拉普拉斯矩阵的特征分解，将高维选择空间映射到低维有效维度d；在特征空间中应用Thompson Sampling，利用相邻节点期望收益相似的图平滑假设。

Result: 理论证明：以高概率获得d*sqrt(T ln N)的遗憾界，其中T为时间范围，N为选择数量，d为有效维度；实验验证：在合成和真实数据上均具有竞争力。

Conclusion: SpectralTS在保持与已知结果相当的遗憾界的同时，提供了计算效率更高的替代方案，特别适用于图结构平滑的实际应用场景。

Abstract: Thompson Sampling (TS) has attracted a lot of interest due to its good empirical performance, in particular in the computational advertising. Though successful, the tools for its performance analysis appeared only recently. In this paper, we describe and analyze SpectralTS algorithm for a bandit problem, where the payoffs of the choices are smooth given an underlying graph. In this setting, each choice is a node of a graph and the expected payoffs of the neighboring nodes are assumed to be similar. Although the setting has application both in recommender systems and advertising, the traditional algorithms would scale poorly with the number of choices. For that purpose we consider an effective dimension d, which is small in real-world graphs. We deliver the analysis showing that the regret of SpectralTS scales as d*sqrt(T ln N) with high probability, where T is the time horizon and N is the number of choices. Since a d*sqrt(T ln N) regret is comparable to the known results, SpectralTS offers a computationally more efficient alternative. We also show that our algorithm is competitive on both synthetic and real-world data.

</details>


### [108] [When Reasoning Models Hurt Behavioral Simulation: A Solver-Sampler Mismatch in Multi-Agent LLM Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.11840)
*Sandro Andric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现，在模拟有限理性行为时，增强推理能力的大语言模型可能成为更好的问题解决者但更差的模拟器，导致过度优化、妥协行为消失和多样性-保真度不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型越来越多地用于社会、经济和政策模拟，普遍假设更强的推理能力会提高模拟保真度。但本文认为，当目标是模拟有限理性行为而非解决战略问题时，这一假设可能不成立。

Method: 研究了三种多智能体谈判环境：模糊碎片化权威交易限制场景、模糊统一反对交易限制场景、以及新的电网削减紧急电力管理案例。比较了三种反思条件（无反思、有限反思、原生推理），在两个主要模型家族上进行测试，并将相同协议扩展到GPT-4.1和GPT-5.2的直接运行。

Result: 在所有三个实验中，有限反思比无反思或原生推理产生更多样化和妥协导向的轨迹。在OpenAI扩展中，GPT-5.2原生推理在45次运行中全部以权威决策结束，而GPT-5.2有限反思在每个环境中都恢复了妥协结果。

Conclusion: 模型能力和模拟保真度是不同的目标，行为模拟应将模型视为采样器而不仅仅是问题解决者。这不是声称推理通常有害，而是方法学警告：在模拟有限理性行为时，需要适当限制模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as agents in social, economic, and policy simulations. A common assumption is that stronger reasoning should improve simulation fidelity. We argue that this assumption can fail when the objective is not to solve a strategic problem, but to sample plausible boundedly rational behavior. In such settings, reasoning-enhanced models can become better solvers and worse simulators: they can over-optimize for strategically dominant actions, collapse compromise-oriented terminal behavior, and sometimes exhibit a diversity-without-fidelity pattern in which local variation survives without outcome-level fidelity. We study this solver-sampler mismatch in three multi-agent negotiation environments adapted from earlier simulation work: an ambiguous fragmented-authority trading-limits scenario, an ambiguous unified-opposition trading-limits scenario, and a new-domain grid-curtailment case in emergency electricity management. We compare three reflection conditions, no reflection, bounded reflection, and native reasoning, across two primary model families and then extend the same protocol to direct OpenAI runs with GPT-4.1 and GPT-5.2. Across all three experiments, bounded reflection produces substantially more diverse and compromise-oriented trajectories than either no reflection or native reasoning. In the direct OpenAI extension, GPT-5.2 native ends in authority decisions in 45 of 45 runs across the three experiments, while GPT-5.2 bounded recovers compromise outcomes in every environment. The contribution is not a claim that reasoning is generally harmful. It is a methodological warning: model capability and simulation fidelity are different objectives, and behavioral simulation should qualify models as samplers, not only as solvers.

</details>


### [109] [Online learning with noisy side observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13740)
*Tomáš Kocák,Gergely Neu,Michal Valko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的部分可观测在线学习模型，其中学习者除了自身损失外，还能观察到其他动作的噪声反馈，反馈质量由加权有向图结构决定。开发了参数无关的高效算法，实现了$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{α^* T})$的遗憾界，其中$α^*$是新的图属性"有效独立数"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有部分可观测在线学习模型通常假设学习者只能观察到所选动作的损失，或者在某些特定结构下观察到有限反馈。本文旨在扩展这一框架，允许学习者在更一般的加权图结构下观察到其他动作的噪声反馈，从而更好地建模现实世界中的信息传播和反馈共享场景。

Method: 提出了一种新的部分可观测模型，其中反馈结构由加权有向图表示，边权重反映了节点间反馈共享的质量。开发了一个完全参数无关的高效算法，该算法不需要知道（甚至不需要估计）图属性$α^*$（有效独立数）。算法通过自适应地探索图结构来收集信息，同时最小化累积损失。

Result: 算法在T轮后实现了$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{α^* T})$的遗憾界，其中$α^*$是图的有效独立数。对于二元边权重的特殊情况，该模型退化为Mannor和Shamir（2011）以及Alon等人（2013）的部分可观测模型，算法恢复了接近最优的遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个更一般的部分可观测在线学习框架，其中反馈结构由加权有向图描述。开发的高效算法在不需要任何参数知识的情况下实现了最优的遗憾界，统一并扩展了先前的工作，为处理具有复杂反馈结构的在线学习问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We propose a new partial-observability model for online learning problems where the learner, besides its own loss, also observes some noisy feedback about the other actions, depending on the underlying structure of the problem. We represent this structure by a weighted directed graph, where the edge weights are related to the quality of the feedback shared by the connected nodes. Our main contribution is an efficient algorithm that guarantees a regret of $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{α^* T})$ after $T$ rounds, where $α^*$ is a novel graph property that we call the effective independence number. Our algorithm is completely parameter-free and does not require knowledge (or even estimation) of $α^*$. For the special case of binary edge weights, our setting reduces to the partial-observability models of Mannor and Shamir (2011) and Alon et al. (2013) and our algorithm recovers the near-optimal regret bounds.

</details>


### [110] [The Long Delay to Arithmetic Generalization: When Learned Representations Outrun Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13082)
*Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现Transformer在算法任务上的"顿悟"现象源于解码器瓶颈而非结构学习失败，通过移植编码器实验和基数选择分析揭示了表示对齐对学习效率的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在理解Transformer在算法任务训练中出现的"顿悟"现象——训练集拟合与突然泛化之间存在长时间延迟的原因。现有研究对这种延迟的根源理解不足，本文试图探究是结构学习失败还是访问已学习结构受限导致了这种延迟。

Method: 研究使用编码器-解码器算术模型，以一阶Collatz预测为任务。通过因果干预实验：1)移植训练好的编码器到新模型；2)移植训练好的解码器；3)冻结收敛的编码器仅重训练解码器。同时考察15种不同基数表示对学习的影响，分析表示对齐与任务算术结构的关系。

Result: 1)编码器在前几千步训练中已组织好奇偶性和余数结构，而输出准确率在数万步内保持接近随机；2)移植训练编码器使顿悟加速2.75倍，移植训练解码器反而有害；3)冻结收敛编码器仅训练解码器完全消除平台期，达到97.6%准确率（联合训练为86.1%）；4)基数选择显著影响学习：与Collatz映射算术对齐的基数（如24进制）达到99.8%准确率，而二进制完全失败；5)基数作为归纳偏置控制解码器可利用的局部数字结构量。

Conclusion: Transformer在算法任务上的顿悟延迟主要源于解码器瓶颈，而非结构学习失败。编码器能快速学习任务结构，但解码器访问这些结构受限。表示对齐（基数选择）作为关键归纳偏置，通过控制局部数字结构的可利用性，显著影响从相同底层任务中的学习效率。

Abstract: Grokking in transformers trained on algorithmic tasks is characterized by a long delay between training-set fit and abrupt generalization, but the source of that delay remains poorly understood. In encoder-decoder arithmetic models, we argue that this delay reflects limited access to already learned structure rather than failure to acquire that structure in the first place. We study one-step Collatz prediction and find that the encoder organizes parity and residue structure within the first few thousand training steps, while output accuracy remains near chance for tens of thousands more. Causal interventions support the decoder bottleneck hypothesis. Transplanting a trained encoder into a fresh model accelerates grokking by 2.75 times, while transplanting a trained decoder actively hurts. Freezing a converged encoder and retraining only the decoder eliminates the plateau entirely and yields 97.6% accuracy, compared to 86.1% for joint training. What makes the decoder's job harder or easier depends on numeral representation. Across 15 bases, those whose factorization aligns with the Collatz map's arithmetic (e.g., base 24) reach 99.8% accuracy, while binary fails completely because its representations collapse and never recover. The choice of base acts as an inductive bias that controls how much local digit structure the decoder can exploit, producing large differences in learnability from the same underlying task.

</details>


### [111] [Momentum Further Constrains Sharpness at the Edge of Stochastic Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.14108)
*Arseniy Andreyev,Advikar Ananthkumar,Marc Walden,Tomaso Poggio,Pierfrancesco Beneventano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SGD with momentum exhibits两种不同的稳定性机制：小批量时动量放大随机波动，倾向于平坦区域；大批量时动量恢复经典稳定效应，倾向于尖锐区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量和小批量梯度在深度学习中广泛应用，但尚不清楚它们是否在类似的稳定性边界机制下运行。需要理解动量如何影响SGD的稳定性行为，特别是批量大小如何调节这种效应。

Method: 通过分析SGD with momentum的Edge of Stochastic Stability (EoSS)机制，研究批量锐度(Batch Sharpness)在不同批量大小下的收敛行为。使用线性稳定性阈值分析动量调整后的稳定性边界。

Result: 批量锐度稳定在两个不同机制：小批量时收敛到较低平台2(1-β)/η，动量放大随机波动，倾向于更平坦区域；大批量时收敛到较高平台2(1+β)/η，动量恢复经典稳定效应，倾向于更尖锐区域，与全批量动态一致。

Conclusion: 动量SGD的稳定性行为是批量大小依赖的，不能由单一动量调整的稳定性阈值解释。这种双重机制对超参数调优和耦合有重要影响，小批量时动量促进平坦解，大批量时促进尖锐解。

Abstract: Recent work suggests that (stochastic) gradient descent self-organizes near an instability boundary, shaping both optimization and the solutions found. Momentum and mini-batch gradients are widely used in practical deep learning optimization, but it remains unclear whether they operate in a comparable regime of instability. We demonstrate that SGD with momentum exhibits an Edge of Stochastic Stability (EoSS)-like regime with batch-size-dependent behavior that cannot be explained by a single momentum-adjusted stability threshold. Batch Sharpness (the expected directional mini-batch curvature) stabilizes in two distinct regimes: at small batch sizes it converges to a lower plateau $2(1-β)/η$, reflecting amplification of stochastic fluctuations by momentum and favoring flatter regions than vanilla SGD; at large batch sizes it converges to a higher plateau $2(1+β)/η$, where momentum recovers its classical stabilizing effect and favors sharper regions consistent with full-batch dynamics. We further show that this aligns with linear stability thresholds and discuss the implications for hyperparameter tuning and coupling.

</details>


### [112] [Adaptive Memory Crystallization for Autonomous AI Agent Learning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13085)
*Rajat Khanda,Mohammad Baqar Sambuddha Chakrabarti,Satyasaran Changdar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应记忆结晶（AMC）架构，用于连续强化学习中的渐进经验巩固，通过液态-玻璃态-晶态三阶段记忆层次模拟记忆结晶过程，显著提升前向迁移、减少灾难性遗忘并降低内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI智能体在动态环境中面临持续学习挑战：如何在获取新能力的同时不遗忘先前知识。现有方法在灾难性遗忘和内存效率方面存在不足，需要一种能够渐进巩固经验的记忆架构。

Method: 提出自适应记忆结晶（AMC）架构，受突触标记与捕获理论启发，将记忆建模为连续结晶过程。采用液态-玻璃态-晶态三阶段记忆层次，由伊藤随机微分方程（SDE）控制，其群体行为由显式Fokker-Planck方程描述，具有闭式Beta稳态分布。

Result: 在Meta-World MT50、Atari 20-game顺序学习和MuJoCo连续运动任务上的实验表明：前向迁移提升34-43%，灾难性遗忘减少67-80%，内存占用降低62%。理论证明包括SDE适定性和全局收敛性、结晶状态指数收敛性、Q学习误差界和内存容量下界。

Conclusion: AMC通过模拟记忆结晶过程，为连续强化学习提供了有效的渐进经验巩固机制，在理论保证和实证性能方面均优于现有基线，为解决灾难性遗忘和内存效率问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: Autonomous AI agents operating in dynamic environments face a persistent challenge: acquiring new capabilities without erasing prior knowledge. We present Adaptive Memory Crystallization (AMC), a memory architecture for progressive experience consolidation in continual reinforcement learning.
  AMC is conceptually inspired by the qualitative structure of synaptic tagging and capture (STC) theory, the idea that memories transition through discrete stability phases, but makes no claim to model the underlying molecular or synaptic mechanisms.
  AMC models memory as a continuous crystallization process in which experiences migrate from plastic to stable states according to a multi-objective utility signal. The framework introduces a three-phase memory hierarchy (Liquid--Glass--Crystal) governed by an Itô stochastic differential equation (SDE) whose population-level behavior is captured by an explicit Fokker--Planck equation admitting a closed-form Beta stationary distribution.
